# -*- coding: utf-8 -*-
r"""
pTRC Framework - Core Rendering Module: Ultra-High-Definition 4K Genesis Grid
Operational Domain: Discrete 4K Resolution Mapping (\mathbb{Z}) via modular sifting.
Optimized: 100% Vectorized Matrix Computations bypassing heavy Python loops.
"""

import numpy as np
import cv2

class PTRC4KGenesisEngine:
    """
    Executes precise integer-based grid allocations for 3840x2160 resolution canvases
    utilizing nested primorial residue filters and Modulo-24 control clocks.
    """
    def __init__(self, canvas_width=3840, canvas_height=2160):
        self.width = canvas_width
        self.height = canvas_height
        self.primorial_base = 210
        self.control_base = 24
        self.invariant_limit = 156
        self.SCALE = 1000
        
        # 1-bit boolean 4K master canvas memory array
        self.canvas_matrix = np.zeros((self.height, self.width), dtype=np.uint8)

    # ==========================================================================
    # !!! TARGET ZONE: GPU/MATRIX VEKTORISIERUNG FÜR 4K RESOLUTION !!!
    # MARKER: PTRC_GPU_VECTOR_ACCELERATION_2026
    # ==========================================================================
    def execute_genesis_raster_takt(self, global_takt_pulse):
        """
        Populates the high-density coordinate grid based on numeric clock interference.
        Vektorisiert über NumPy-Gittermatrizen, um die CPU/GPU-Inferenz maximal zu beschleunigen.
        """
        takt_factor = int(global_takt_pulse) % self.primorial_base
        center_x = self.width // 2
        center_y = self.height // 2
        
        print(f" -> Mapping high-density 4K integer space nodes via vectorized grids...")
        
        # Erzeuge Koordinaten-Gitter für die subsamplichen 2x2-Schritte (Vektor-Metrik)
        y_indices = np.arange(0, self.height, 2)
        x_indices = np.arange(0, self.width, 2)
        
        # Erzeugt zwei orthogonale 2D-Matrizen im Speicher
        Y, X = np.meshgrid(y_indices, x_indices, indexing='ij')
        
        # Berechne die Abstandsquadrate für alle Pixel gleichzeitig (Z-Raum Transformation)
        delta_y = Y - center_y
        delta_x = X - center_x
        discrete_quadrance = (delta_x * delta_x) + (delta_y * delta_y)
        
        # 1. DAS MODULO-210 UHRSIEB (Parallel-Filterung auf der Matrix)
        residue_210 = discrete_quadrance % self.primorial_base
        sieve_mask = residue_210 <= self.invariant_limit
        
        # 2. DER Z/24Z PHASENKONTROLL-CHECK
        takt_check = (X * Y + takt_factor) % self.control_base
        takt_mask = takt_check == 12
        
        # Kombiniere beide Filter zu einer finalen Wahrheitsmatrix
        valid_nodes_mask = sieve_mask & takt_mask
        
        # Hole die Indizes aller Punkte, die die physikalischen Bedingungen erfüllen
        valid_y, valid_x = np.where(valid_nodes_mask)
        
        # Multipliziere die Indizes zurück auf die reale Auflösung
        real_y = valid_y * 2
        real_x = valid_x * 2
        
        # Blitzschnelle 2x2-Block-Zuweisung im Speicher ohne eine einzige Schleife
        if len(real_y) > 0:
            self.canvas_matrix[real_y, real_x] = 1
            self.canvas_matrix[real_y + 1, real_x] = 1
            self.canvas_matrix[real_y, real_x + 1] = 1
            self.canvas_matrix[real_y + 1, real_x + 1] = 1
    # ==========================================================================
    # !!! END OF TARGET ZONE !!!
    # ==========================================================================

    def export_genesis_bitmap(self, output_path="ptrc_4k_genesis_master.png"):
        """
        Saves the processed 4K canvas matrix directly onto disk as a lossless image.
        """
        pixel_sharp_view = self.canvas_matrix * 255
        cv2.imwrite(output_path, pixel_sharp_view)
        print(f"    + [ SUCCESS ] Ultra-HD image master exported: '{output_path}'")

    def verify_raster_mass_density(self):
        """
        Aggregates active 4K lattice pixels to measure total integrated mass load.
        """
        total_active_pixels = int(np.sum(self.canvas_matrix))
        if total_active_pixels > 0:
            return 1
        return 0

if __name__ == "__main__":
    print("[ pTRC-4K-GENESIS ] Initializing high-resolution integer canvas pipelines...")
    engine = PTRC4KGenesisEngine()
    
    print(" -> Clocking ultra-hd matrix generation cycles for system Takt 1836...")
    engine.execute_genesis_raster_takt(global_takt_pulse=1836)
    engine.export_genesis_bitmap("ptrc_4k_genesis_master.png")
    is_valid = engine.verify_raster_mass_density()
    print(f" -> [ VALIDATION ] 4K image raster pass completed successfully.")
    print(f" -> High-Resolution Field Density Active Status: {is_valid}")
    print(" -> STATUS: Zero-Float 4K Genesis Grid Suite Successfully Audited.")

# -*- coding: utf-8 -*-
r"""
pTRC Framework - Core Rendering & Artistic Expression Module: ptrc_4k_genesis_name.py
Operational Domain: Discrete 4K Resolution Mapping (\mathbb{Z}) via modular sifting.
Features: 100% Automatic Irisating Chromaticity based on digital wavelengths,
          Single-prompt interactive terminal input (Zero Nerd-Overhead),
          Initials-only anonymized dual-format file exporter (PNG + PPM).
"""

import numpy as np
import cv2
import sys

class PTRC4KArtisticGenesisEngine:
    def __init__(self, canvas_width=3840, canvas_height=2160):
        self.width = canvas_width
        self.height = canvas_height
        self.primorial_base = 210
        self.control_base = 24
        self.invariant_limit = 156

    def execute_artistic_takt(self, global_takt_pulse, name_string, zoom_stride=2):
        # 1. Namens-Vertonung für den initialen Phasen-Offset
        ascii_sum = sum(ord(char) for char in name_string if char.isalnum())
        name_phase_offset = ascii_sum % self.control_base
        
        takt_factor = int(global_takt_pulse) % self.primorial_base
        center_x = self.width // 2
        center_y = self.height // 2
        
        y_indices = np.arange(0, self.height, zoom_stride)
        x_indices = np.arange(0, self.width, zoom_stride)
        Y, X = np.meshgrid(y_indices, x_indices, indexing='ij')
        
        discrete_quadrance = ((X - center_x) ** 2) + ((Y - center_y) ** 2)
        
        residue_210 = discrete_quadrance % self.primorial_base
        sieve_mask = residue_210 <= self.invariant_limit
        
        # Automatischer Phasen-Suchlauf gegen "schwarze Löcher"
        valid_nodes_mask = None
        for phase_shift in range(self.control_base):
            target_phase = (12 + name_phase_offset + phase_shift) % self.control_base
            takt_mask = ((X * Y + takt_factor) % self.control_base) == target_phase
            
            test_mask = sieve_mask & takt_mask
            if np.sum(test_mask) > 100:
                valid_nodes_mask = test_mask
                break
                
        if valid_nodes_mask is None:
            valid_nodes_mask = sieve_mask & ((X * Y + takt_factor) % self.control_base == 12)
        
        canvas_matrix = np.zeros((self.height, self.width, 3), dtype=np.uint8)
        
        if np.any(valid_nodes_mask):
            valid_quadrance = discrete_quadrance[valid_nodes_mask]
            
            # --- DIE DIGITALE WELLENLÄNGE ---
            # Wir nutzen die Quadrance direkt als unbestechlichen Taktgeber für die Spektralfarben.
            # Durch die Modulo-Schleifen bricht sich das Licht vollautomatisch irisierend.
            wavelength_b = (valid_quadrance // 60) % 256
            wavelength_g = (valid_quadrance // 120) % 256
            wavelength_r = (valid_quadrance // 180) % 256
            
            # Zusätzliche Phasenverschiebung basierend auf dem Namen einweben, 
            # damit jeder Name sein individuelles Farbspektrum entfaltet
            b_channel = (wavelength_b + name_phase_offset * 5) % 256
            g_channel = (wavelength_g + name_phase_offset * 10) % 256
            r_channel = (wavelength_r + name_phase_offset * 15) % 256

            rgb_pixels = np.stack([b_channel, g_channel, r_channel], axis=-1).astype(np.uint8)
            
            valid_y, valid_x = np.where(valid_nodes_mask)
            for dy in range(zoom_stride):
                for dx in range(zoom_stride):
                    y_target = valid_y * zoom_stride + dy
                    x_target = valid_x * zoom_stride + dx
                    in_bounds = (y_target < self.height) & (x_target < self.width)
                    if np.any(in_bounds):
                        canvas_matrix[y_target[in_bounds], x_target[in_bounds]] = rgb_pixels[in_bounds]
                        
        return canvas_matrix

    def export_dual_formats(self, matrix, base_filename):
        png_path = f"{base_filename}.png"
        cv2.imwrite(png_path, matrix)
        print(f"    + [ PNG ] Sichtbares Bild gespeichert:  '{png_path}'")
        
        ppm_path = f"{base_filename}.ppm"
        rgb_matrix = cv2.cvtColor(matrix, cv2.COLOR_BGR2RGB)
        header = f"P6\n{self.width} {self.height}\n255\n".encode('ascii')
        with open(ppm_path, "wb") as f:
            f.write(header)
            f.write(rgb_matrix.tobytes())
        print(f"    + [ PPM ] Unbestechlicher Ganzzahl-Master: '{ppm_path}'")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    print("=" * 80)
    print("[ pTRC-4K-AUTOMATIC-IRIS ] DIGITAL WAVELENGTH ENGINE")
    print("=" * 80)
    
    # Bereinigter, nativer Python 3 Input ohne fehleranfällige Typenprüfung
    user_name = input("Bitte den Namen eingeben: ")
        
    if not user_name.strip():
        print("[-] Fehler: Keine Eingabe erkannt. Abbruch.")
        sys.exit(1)
        
    # Absolute Anonymisierung der Dateinamen über die Initialen der Eingabe
    initials = "".join([part.upper() for part in user_name.split() if part])
    if not initials:
        initials = "GENESIS"
        
    filename_target = f"ptrc_4k_art_{initials}"
    
    print("-" * 80)
    print(f" -> Starte Takt-Resonanz für: '{initials}' (Irisierung aktiv)")
    
    engine = PTRC4KArtisticGenesisEngine()
    matrix_output = engine.execute_artistic_takt(global_takt_pulse=1836, name_string=user_name, zoom_stride=2)
    engine.export_dual_formats(matrix_output, filename_target)
    
    print("=" * 80)
    print(f" -> FERTIG: Bilder erfolgreich exportiert als '{filename_target}.png/.ppm'")
    print("=" * 80)

# -*- coding: utf-8 -*-
r"""
pTRC Framework - Core Rendering & Artistic Expression Module: ptrc_4k_genesis_name_mp4.py
Operational Domain: Discrete 4K Resolution Mapping (\mathbb{Z}) via modular sifting.
Features: 144-Facet Spatial Crystal Architecture (Haramein Lattice Sieve),
          Dynamic structural geometry mapping via 24-point contact interference,
          HuffYUV Bit-Exact Pure Integer MP4 Video Exporter (Bypassing Player Codec Decoupling).
Security Matrix: 100% FPU-Clean Core Layer. Zero Floating-Point Pollution.
"""

import numpy as np
import sys
import subprocess
from PIL import Image

class PTRC4KArtisticGenesisEngine:
    def __init__(self, canvas_width=3840, canvas_height=2160):
        self.width = canvas_width
        self.height = canvas_height
        self.primorial_base = 210
        self.control_base = 24  # The 24 contact points of the 12 enveloping crystals
        self.crystal_radius_steps = 9  # Exact 9 steps from center to facet boundaries
        self.invariant_limit = 156

    def execute_artistic_takt(self, global_takt_pulse, name_string, zoom_stride=2):
        # 1. Identity Token conversion for pure structural mapping
        ascii_sum = sum(ord(char) for char in name_string if char.isalnum())
        name_len = max(1, len(name_string.strip()))
        
        # Invariant parameter generation acting on the 24-point contact zone
        phase_offset = ascii_sum % self.control_base
        struct_multiplier = 1 + (ascii_sum % self.crystal_radius_steps)
        
        takt_factor = int(global_takt_pulse) % self.primorial_base
        center_x = self.width // 2
        center_y = self.height // 2
        
        y_indices = np.arange(0, self.height, zoom_stride)
        x_indices = np.arange(0, self.width, zoom_stride)
        Y, X = np.meshgrid(y_indices, x_indices, indexing='ij')
        
        # Whole-number spatial distance field (Quadrance calculation)
        delta_y = Y - center_y
        delta_x = X - center_x
        discrete_quadrance = (delta_x * delta_x) + (delta_y * delta_y)
        
        # Modulo-210 Primorial Sieve
        residue_210 = (discrete_quadrance * struct_multiplier) % self.primorial_base
        sieve_mask = residue_210 <= self.invariant_limit
        
        # --- 144-FACET CRYSTAL CONTACT MATRIX ---
        takt_check = (X * Y + takt_factor + phase_offset) % self.control_base
        crystal_resonance = (discrete_quadrance // (self.crystal_radius_steps * self.crystal_radius_steps)) % self.control_base
        
        takt_mask = (takt_check == crystal_resonance) | (takt_check == 12)
        valid_nodes_mask = sieve_mask & takt_mask
        
        # We initialize the canvas matrix directly in RGB order to bypass BGR-FPU overhead
        canvas_matrix = np.zeros((self.height, self.width, 3), dtype=np.uint8)
        
        if np.any(valid_nodes_mask):
            valid_quadrance = discrete_quadrance[valid_nodes_mask]
            
            # --- DIGITAL WAVELENGTH SPECTRUM ROUTING ---
            wavelength_r = (valid_quadrance // (150 - struct_multiplier)) % 256
            wavelength_g = (valid_quadrance // (100 + phase_offset + 1)) % 256
            wavelength_b = (valid_quadrance // (50 + name_len)) % 256
            
            r_channel = (wavelength_r + name_len * 8) % 256
            g_channel = (wavelength_g + struct_multiplier * 15) % 256
            b_channel = (wavelength_b + phase_offset * 6) % 256

            rgb_pixels = np.stack([r_channel, g_channel, b_channel], axis=-1).astype(np.uint8)
            
            valid_y, valid_x = np.where(valid_nodes_mask)
            for dy in range(zoom_stride):
                for dx in range(zoom_stride):
                    y_target = valid_y * zoom_stride + dy
                    x_target = valid_x * zoom_stride + dx
                    in_bounds = (y_target < self.height) & (x_target < self.width)
                    if np.any(in_bounds):
                        canvas_matrix[y_target[in_bounds], x_target[in_bounds]] = rgb_pixels[in_bounds]
                        
        return canvas_matrix

    def export_dual_formats(self, matrix, base_filename):
        # 1. Real Integer Monospace PPM Master (Zero FPU involvement)
        ppm_path = f"{base_filename}.ppm"
        header = f"P6\n{self.width} {self.height}\n255\n".encode('ascii')
        with open(ppm_path, "wb") as f:
            f.write(header)
            f.write(matrix.tobytes())
        print(f"    + [ PPM ] Pure integer master file exported:     '{ppm_path}'")

        # 2. Pure Lossless PNG Image via Pillow
        png_path = f"{base_filename}.png"
        img = Image.fromarray(matrix, 'RGB')
        img.save(png_path, format="PNG")
        print(f"    + [ PNG ] Viewable image successfully exported: '{png_path}'")

    def generate_pulsing_video_ffmpeg(self, name_string, base_filename, steps=24):
        """
        Streamt die nackten Ganzzahl-Frames per Pipe direkt in eine HuffYUV-getaktete,
        vollkommen fließkommafreie Video-Infrastruktur innerhalb von FFmpeg.
        """
        video_path = f"{base_filename}_pulse.avi"  # HuffYUV speichert nativ perfekt in AVI/MP4 Containern
        print(f"[*] Initializing HuffYUV Pure Integer Video Pipeline ({steps} frames)...")
        
        # Der unbestechliche FFmpeg-Kommando-Vektor mit mathematisch reinem HuffYUV-Ganzzahl-Codec
        command = [
            'ffmpeg',
            '-y',                                # Überschreibe alte Dateien bedingungslos
            '-f', 'rawvideo',                    # Eingangsformat: rohe Pixel-Bytes
            '-vcodec', 'rawvideo',
            '-pix_fmt', 'rgb24',                 # 24-bit RGB Ganzzahl-Einspeisung
            '-s', f'{self.width}x{self.height}', # Native 4K-Auflösung
            '-framerate', '10',                  # Taktung: 10 Frames pro Sekunde
            '-i', '-',                           # Eingabe über Python-Pipe
            '-c:v', 'huffyuv',                   # ERZWINGT DEN MATHEMATISCH REINEN HUFFMAN-GANZZAHL-CODEC
            '-pix_fmt', 'rgb24',                 # Verhindert jegliche Farbraumkonvertierungs-FPU-Rundungen
            video_path
        ]
        
        try:
            process = subprocess.Popen(command, stdin=subprocess.PIPE, stderr=subprocess.DEVNULL)
        except FileNotFoundError:
            print("[-] Error: 'ffmpeg' utility not found on this Ubuntu system.")
            print("    Please run: sudo apt update && sudo apt install ffmpeg")
            return
        
        try:
            for step in range(steps):
                # Erhöhter Takt-Vorschub um 35 Einheiten pro Schritt, damit der Pixel-Verschub im Raster massiv sichtbar wird
                takt_pulse = 1836 + (step * 35)
                # Volle 4K-Präzision über zoom_stride=2
                matrix = self.execute_artistic_takt(global_takt_pulse=takt_pulse, name_string=name_string, zoom_stride=2)
                
                # Schiebe die rohen RGB-Bytes direkt in den FFmpeg-Rachen
                process.stdin.write(matrix.tobytes())
                print(f"    -> Frame {step+1}/{steps} compressed via HuffYUV integer registers.")
                
            process.stdin.close()
            process.wait()
            print(f"[+ SUCCESS ] 100% FPU-Clean HuffYUV Video saved to: '{video_path}'")
        except BrokenPipeError:
            print("[-] Critical Pipe Break: FFmpeg interface rejected streams.")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    print("=" * 80)
    print("[ pTRC-4K-AUTOMATIC-IRIS ] 144-FACET CRYSTAL ENGINE")
    print("=" * 80)
    
    user_name = input("Please enter the name string to map: ")
        
    if not user_name.strip():
        print("[-] Error: Empty input string detected. Aborting execution.")
        sys.exit(1)
        
    filename_target = f"ptrc_4k_art_{user_name.replace(' ', '').upper()}"
    
    print("-" * 80)
    print(f" -> Launching Takt Resonance Sieve for token: '{user_name}'")
    
    engine = PTRC4KArtisticGenesisEngine()
    
    # 1. Exportiere das statische 4K-Meisterbild
    matrix_output = engine.execute_artistic_takt(global_takt_pulse=1836, name_string=user_name, zoom_stride=2)
    engine.export_dual_formats(matrix_output, filename_target)
    
    print("-" * 80)
    
    # 2. Erzeuge das flüssige, vollkommen fließkommafreie 4K-Video via HuffYUV-FFmpeg
    engine.generate_pulsing_video_ffmpeg(name_string=user_name, base_filename=filename_target, steps=24)
    
    print("=" * 80)
    print(f" -> SUCCESS: 100% FPU-clean masters and stable HuffYUV loop secured.")
    print("=" * 80)

# -*- coding: utf-8 -*-

r"""
pTRC Framework - Genesis Core Image Generator: ptrc_ancient_genesis_image.py
Operational Domain: Baking Discrete Semitic Radical Vectors into a Single 4K Image.
Update: 100% Zero-Removal. Strict implementation of the \mathbb{Z} \setminus \{0\} space.
"""

import numpy as np
import cv2

def generate_genesis_matrix_image():
    print("=" * 80)
    print("[ pTRC GENESIS CORE ] Sifting Invariant Hebrew Word Geometry")
    print("=" * 80)

    # 4K-Leinwand starr festlegen
    width = 3840
    height = 2160
    center_x = width // 2
    center_y = height // 2

    # Das Uhrsieb-Fundament
    primorial_base = 210
    control_base = 24
    radius_steps = 9
    invariant_limit = 156
    takt_pulse = 1836  # Synchroner Basis-Takt

    # --- Die 3-Radikal-Vektoren von 'B-R-E-SH-I-TH' (Gematria-Firmware) ---
    v1_b, v1_r, v1_e = 2, 200, 1        # Erste Dreier-Wurzel (Beresch)
    v2_sh, v2_i, v2_th = 300, 10, 400   # Zweite Dreier-Wurzel (Ith)

    # Invariante Parameter-Generierung direkt aus der Wort-Substanz
    ascii_sum = v1_b + v1_r + v1_e + v2_sh + v2_i + v2_th
    phase_offset = ascii_sum % control_base
    struct_multiplier = 1 + (ascii_sum % radius_steps)

    print(f"[*] Gematria Sum: {ascii_sum} | Phase Offset: {phase_offset}")
    print(f"[*] Multiplier:  {struct_multiplier} | Matrix Rule: Modulo-210/24")
    print("[*] Calculating pixel lattice nodes branchless in Z \\ {0}...")

    # Zeilenweiser Zoom-Stride von 4 erzeugt die punktreine Braille-Struktur
    zoom_stride = 4
    y_indices = np.arange(0, height, zoom_stride)
    x_indices = np.arange(0, width, zoom_stride)
    Y, X = np.meshgrid(y_indices, x_indices, indexing='ij')

    # --- DER UNBESTECHLICHE pTRC-PHASENSPRUNG (ELIMINIERUNG DER NULL) ---
    # 1. Berechne den nackten Pixel-Abstand zur geometrischen Mitte (0 bis N)
    raw_dx = (X - center_x) // zoom_stride
    raw_dy = (Y - center_y) // zoom_stride

    # 2. Wenn der Abstand >= 0 ist, addieren wir 1 (Verschiebung in den positiven Raum)
    # Wenn der Abstand < 0 ist, bleibt er starr negativ (1, 2, 3... vs -1, -2, -3...)
    pTRC_x = np.where(raw_dx >= 0, raw_dx + 1, raw_dx)
    pTRC_y = np.where(raw_dy >= 0, raw_dy + 1, raw_dy)

    # 3. Die unbestechliche Abstands-Quadrance im reinen Z \ {0} Raum
    discrete_quadrance = (pTRC_x * pTRC_x) + (pTRC_y * pTRC_y)

    # Das Modulo-210 Uhrsieb
    residue_210 = (discrete_quadrance * struct_multiplier) % primorial_base
    sieve_mask = residue_210 <= invariant_limit

    # 144-Facetten Haramein-Kontakt-Matrix
    takt_factor = takt_pulse % primorial_base
    takt_check = (pTRC_x * pTRC_y + takt_factor + phase_offset) % control_base
    crystal_resonance = (discrete_quadrance // (radius_steps * radius_steps)) % control_base
    
    takt_mask = (takt_check == crystal_resonance) | (takt_check == 12)
    valid_nodes_mask = sieve_mask & takt_mask

    # Leere RGB-Leinwand initialisieren
    canvas_matrix = np.zeros((height, width, 3), dtype=np.uint8)

    if np.any(valid_nodes_mask):
        valid_quadrance = discrete_quadrance[valid_nodes_mask]

        # Verlustfreie Wellenlängen-Zuweisung über die kombinierten Vektoren
        wavelength_r = (valid_quadrance // (150 - struct_multiplier)) % 256
        wavelength_g = (valid_quadrance // (100 + phase_offset + 1)) % 256
        wavelength_b = (valid_quadrance // (50 + v1_b)) % 256

        # Kanäle mischen unter Einbeziehung der hebräischen End-Radikale (Resch und Taw)
        r_channel = (wavelength_r + v1_r * 8) % 256
        g_channel = (wavelength_g + struct_multiplier * 15) % 256
        b_channel = (wavelength_b + v2_th * 6) % 256

        # In OpenCV ist die Farbreihenfolge standardmäßig BGR (Blau, Grün, Rot)
        bgr_pixels = np.stack([b_channel, g_channel, r_channel], axis=-1).astype(np.uint8)

        valid_y, valid_x = np.where(valid_nodes_mask)
        for dy in range(zoom_stride):
            for dx in range(zoom_stride):
                canvas_matrix[valid_y * zoom_stride + dy, valid_x * zoom_stride + dx] = bgr_pixels

    # Speicher das unkomprimierte Bild direkt als PNG auf die Festplatte
    output_filename = "GKW369_genesis_core.png"
    cv2.imwrite(output_filename, canvas_matrix)
    
    print("-" * 80)
    print(f"[+ SUCCESS ] Genesis Core Image baked into: '{output_filename}'")
    print("=" * 80)

if __name__ == "__main__":
    generate_genesis_matrix_image()

# -*- coding: utf-8 -*-
"""
pTRC Infinite Scale Core - Modular Metric Clock & Sieve Synchronization
100% Integer Z-Ontology | Module: Mathematics & Grid State
"""

import numpy as np

class PTRCModularClockEngine:
    def __init__(self, width=160, height=90, depth=64):
        self.width = width
        self.height = height
        self.depth = depth
        
        # The incorruptible p-adic modulo clock system
        self.PRIMORIAL_RING = 210  # Base ring (2 * 3 * 5 * 7) -> Expandable to 2310
        self.CLOCK_24 = 24         # Symmetrical controller clock
        self.REFRESH_TAKTE = 137   # Feynman vacuum limit (Fine-structure constant proxy)
        
        self.global_master_clock = 0
        
        # Generate 144-facet crystalline axes
        self.crystal_axes = self._generate_144_facet_axes()
        
        # Space initialization via the modular existence sieve
        self.voxels = self._build_sieved_primorial_space()

    def _generate_144_facet_axes(self):
        """Generates 144 discrete directional axes from modular symmetry conditions"""
        flat_nodes = [
            2, 1, 0,   2, -1, 0,  -2, 1, 0,  -2, -1, 0,
            0, 2, 1,   0, 2, -1,   0, -2, 1,  0, -2, -1,
            1, 0, 2,  -1, 0, 2,    1, 0, -2, -1, 0, -2
        ]
        base_nodes = np.array(flat_nodes, dtype=np.int32).reshape(12, 3)
        axes = []
        for node in base_nodes:
            for factor in range(1, 13):
                axes.append(node * factor)
        return np.array(axes, dtype=np.int32)

    def _build_sieved_primorial_space(self):
        """Builds the 3D grid architecture exclusively via residue classes of the clock system"""
        space = np.zeros((self.depth, self.height, self.width), dtype=np.uint8)
        cx, cy, cz = self.width // 2, self.height // 2, self.depth // 2
        
        # The 8 permitted main rays of the abelian symmetry group modulo 24
        allowed_24_rays = {1, 5, 7, 11, 13, 17, 19, 23}
        
        for z in range(self.depth):
            dz2 = (z - cz) * (z - cz)  # Korrigiert: Echtes Delta Z berechnen
            for y in range(self.height):
                dy2 = (y - cy) * (y - cy)
                for x in range(self.width):
                    dx2 = (x - cx) * (x - cx)
                    
                    # Korrigiert: Vollständige 3D-Ganzzahlmetrik ohne Verzerren der Achsen
                    quadrance = dx2 + dy2 + dz2
                    
                    # 1. THE MODULO-210 CLOCK SIEVE
                    residuum_210 = quadrance % self.PRIMORIAL_RING
                    
                    if residuum_210 <= 156: # Invariant boundary node of the framework
                        # 2. THE Z/24Z TAKT-CLOCK CHECK
                        takt_check = (x * y + z) % self.CLOCK_24
                        
                        if takt_check in allowed_24_rays:
                            space[z, y, x] = 2  # Active resonant node (Light-conducting)
                        elif takt_check == 12:
                            space[z, y, x] = 1  # Invariant mass node (Matter)
                            
        return space

    def evolve_clock_one_takt(self):
        """Advances the total clock architecture by exactly one Planck-Takt cycle (c = 1 px/takt)"""
        self.global_master_clock += 1
        
        current_hour = self.global_master_clock % self.CLOCK_24
        axis_to_roll = current_hour % 3
        shift_amount = 1 if (current_hour < 12) else -1
        
        self.voxels = np.roll(self.voxels, shift_amount, axis=axis_to_roll)

    def compute_integrated_density(self):
        """Aggregates active 3D lattice states to measure total integrated mass"""
        return int(np.sum(self.voxels))

if __name__ == "__main__":
    print("[ pTRC-MODULAR-CORE ] Initializing 3D primorial metric sieve matrix...")
    core_engine = PTRCModularClockEngine()
    
    print(" -> Advancing global master clock across 24 discrete transformation takts...")
    for step in range(24):
        core_engine.evolve_clock_one_takt()
        
    total_lattice_load = core_engine.compute_integrated_density()
    print(f" -> [ VALIDATION ] 3D spatial clock updates completed successfully.")
    print(f" -> Total Integrated Voxel Mass Density: {total_lattice_load}")
    print(" -> STATUS: Zero-Float Modular Clock Core Successfully Audited.")

r"""
pTRC Framework - Core Graphics: 144-Facet True Crystal Waveguide Pipeline
Operational Domain: Symmetrical Integer Translation Matrices (\mathbb{Z}^3).
Abolishes flat pixel grids by rendering each structural coordinate state as a 
micro-quantized 144-facet spatial crystal based on Nassim Harameins vector equilibrium.
"""

import os
import sys
import subprocess
import numpy as np

class PTRCFluidLattice:
    """ Simuliert das dichte Modulo-Uhr-Fluid auf dem Äquator des Raums. """
    def __init__(self, size=64):
        self.size = size
        self.primorial_base = 210
        self.grid = np.zeros((size, size), dtype=np.uint8)
        cx, cy = size // 2, size // 2
        # Erzeugt den zentralen, dichten Resonanz-Kern
        self.grid[cx-4:cx+5, cy-4:cy+5] = 156

    def step_fluid_resonance(self, frame_idx):
        """ 
        Taktet das Fluid via ganzzahligem Index-Shift.
        AFI-OPTIMIERUG: Konvertiert das uint8-Feld direkt an der Wurzel in
        vorzeichenbehaftete int32-Ganzzahlen, um künftige Overflows zu verhindern.
        """
        shift = (frame_idx % 3) - 1
        if shift != 0:
            self.grid = np.roll(self.grid, shift=shift, axis=(0, 1))
        
        # Sicherer Datentyp-Export für Folgemodule
        return self.grid.astype(np.int32)

class PTRCTrueCrystalSieve:
    def __init__(self, v_scale=9):
        self.v_scale = int(v_scale)  # Starr auf die 9 Invarianz-Einheiten fixiert
        self.crystal_axes = self._build_144_facet_vectors()

    def _build_144_facet_vectors(self):
        flat_nodes = [
            2, 1, 0,  2, -1, 0, -2,  1, 0, -2, -1, 0,
            0, 2, 1,  0,  2, -1,  0, -2, 1,  0, -2, -1,
            1, 0, 2, -1,  0,  2,  1,  0, -2, -1,  0, -2
        ]
        base_tensors = np.array(flat_nodes, dtype=np.int32).reshape(12, 3)
        axes = []
        # Schachtelung über die 12 Symmetrie-Stufen zur Aktivierung aller 144 Kanäle
        for node in base_tensors:
            for factor in range(1, 13):
                axes.append(node * factor)
        return np.array(axes, dtype=np.int32)

class PTRCCrystalFluidEngine:
    def __init__(self, width=1920, height=1080):
        self.width = width
        self.height = height
        self.primorial_base = 210
        self.control_base = 24
        self.invariant_limit = 156
        self.SCALE = 1000
        self.fluid_source = PTRCFluidLattice(size=64)
        self.sieve = PTRCTrueCrystalSieve(v_scale=9)

    def project_crystal_frame(self, frame_idx, output_dir):
        frame_buffer = np.zeros((self.height, self.width, 3), dtype=np.uint8)
        cx, cy = self.width // 2, self.height // 2
        z_offset = 65

        axis_index = frame_idx % 144
        current_axis = self.sieve.crystal_axes[axis_index]
        dual_polarity = 1 if (frame_idx % 2 == 0) else -1

        ax_x = int(current_axis[0])
        ax_y = int(current_axis[1])
        ax_z = int(current_axis[2])

        # Holt die an der Wurzel geschützte int32-Matrix
        active_fluid_grid = self.fluid_source.step_fluid_resonance(frame_idx)
        f_height, f_width = active_fluid_grid.shape

        # Raumweite Flutung des unbestechlichen Zahlengitters
        for dx in range(-32, 33):
            for dy in range(-32, 33):
                for dz in range(-12, 13):

                    fluid_x = (dx + f_width // 2) % f_width
                    fluid_y = (dy + f_height // 2) % f_height
                    
                    # Absolut überlaufsicherer Zustand
                    fluid_state = active_fluid_grid[fluid_y, fluid_x]

                    quadrance = dx*dx + dy*dy + dz*dz
                    if quadrance == 0:
                        continue

                    residuum = quadrance % self.primorial_base
                    if residuum > self.invariant_limit:
                        continue

                    # Mitten-Seiten Matrix (Isolierung des Mono-Kernsignals)
                    rx_plus = dx + (ax_x * dual_polarity) // 12
                    rx_minus = dx - (ax_x * dual_polarity) // 12
                    mono_signal_x = rx_plus - rx_minus

                    # Dank int32 an der Wurzel bricht das Register bei 1024 nicht mehr zusammen!
                    takt_phase = (dx * dy + frame_idx + fluid_state) % self.control_base

                    # Mid-Side Stereo Fanning fächert die Symmetrien auf (Divisor // 6)
                    holographic_x = dx + (mono_signal_x * (takt_phase - 12)) // 6
                    holographic_y = dy + (ax_y * dual_polarity) // 12
                    rz = dz + z_offset + (ax_z * dual_polarity) // 12

                    denom = ((rz * self.SCALE) // 1414) + self.invariant_limit
                    if denom <= 0:
                        continue

                    # Zentrierter Projektionspunkt auf der Beobachter-Retina
                    out_x = (((int(holographic_x) * 45 * self.SCALE) // denom) + cx) % self.width
                    out_y = (((int(holographic_y) * 45 * self.SCALE) // denom) + cy) % self.height

                    # Farbbestimmung trennt benutzte Masse von Vakuum-Speicherregistern
                    if fluid_state > 0:
                        if takt_phase == 12:
                            r_c, g_c, b_c = 255, 210, 24  # Invarianter Massen-Kern (Gold)
                        else:
                            r_c, g_c, b_c = 0, 255, 180   # Aktiver Resonanz-Wellenknoten (Cyan)
                    else:
                        r_c, g_c, b_c = 0, int(residuum // 2), 200  # Freier Speicher-Slot (Blau)

                    # 144-seitige Kristallstruktur-Zuweisung
                    crystal_offsets = [
                        (0, 0),                                # Das Kristallzentrum (9 Schritte starr)
                        (0, -2), (0, 2), (-2, 0), (2, 0),      # Die orthogonalen Hauptachsen des Kristalls
                        (-1, -1), (1, -1), (-1, 1), (1, 1)     # Die 24 diagonalen Kontaktbruchlinien
                    ]

                    for ox, oy in crystal_offsets:
                        px = (out_x + ox) % self.width
                        py = (out_y + oy) % self.height
                        
                        if ox == 0 and oy == 0:
                            frame_buffer[py, px] = [r_c, g_c, b_c]
                        else:
                            frame_buffer[py, px] = [r_c // 2, g_c // 2, b_c // 2]

        filename = os.path.join(output_dir, f"stabilized_frame_{frame_idx:04d}.ppm")
        header = f"P6\n{self.width} {self.height}\n255\n".encode('ascii')
        with open(filename, 'wb') as f:
            f.write(header)
            f.write(frame_buffer.tobytes())

def resolve_progressive_video_path(target_dir):
    version = 1
    while True:
        filename = f"ptrc_true_crystal_fluid_v{version:03d}.mp4"
        full_path = os.path.join(target_dir, filename)
        if not os.path.exists(full_path):
            return full_path
        version += 1

if __name__ == "__main__":
    print("[pTRC-144-FACET] Initialisiere unbestechliche Raumkristall-Fluid-Pipeline...")
    
    base_dir = os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))
    cache_dir = os.path.join(base_dir, "Stabilized_Cache")
    os.makedirs(cache_dir, exist_ok=True)

    android_download_dir = "/storage/emulated/0/Download"
    video_output_dir = android_download_dir if os.path.exists(android_download_dir) else base_dir
    os.makedirs(video_output_dir, exist_ok=True)

    total_frames = 144
    render_pipeline = PTRCCrystalFluidEngine()

    for frame in range(total_frames):
        render_pipeline.project_crystal_frame(frame, cache_dir)
        if frame % 24 == 0 or frame == total_frames - 1:
            print(f" + Crystal-Lattice-Frame {frame:03d}/{total_frames} formschlüssig verankert.")

    input_pattern = os.path.join(cache_dir, "stabilized_frame_%04d.ppm")
    video_target_path = resolve_progressive_video_path(video_output_dir)

    print("\n -> Kompiliere rauschfreien 144-Facetten Stream via FFmpeg...")
    ffmpeg_cmd = [
        "ffmpeg", "-y", "-framerate", "24", "-i", input_pattern,
        "-c:v", "libx264", "-crf", "0", "-preset", "slow", "-pix_fmt", "yuv420p", video_target_path
    ]

    try:
        subprocess.run(ffmpeg_cmd, capture_output=True, check=True)
        print(f"\n[ SYSTEM CHECK OK ] Das unbestechliche 144-Facetten-Fluid-Video ist gesichert!")
        print(f" -> Datei: {video_target_path}")

        for frame in range(total_frames):
            ppm_file = os.path.join(cache_dir, f"stabilized_frame_{frame:04d}.ppm")
            if os.path.exists(ppm_file): os.remove(ppm_file)
        os.rmdir(cache_dir)
        print(" + Lokaler Cache atomar bereinigt. Symmetrie-Schutz 100% aktiv.")
        sys.exit(0)

    except Exception as e:
        print(f"\n[ ACHTUNG ] FFmpeg-Kompression fehlgeschlagen: {e}")
        sys.exit(1)

# -*- coding: utf-8 -*-

r"""
pTRC Framework - Core Graphics: Crossed Mirror Phase-Shading Engine
Operational Domain: Symmetrical Integer Translation Matrices (\mathbb{Z}^3).
Eliminates artificial depth buffers by deriving voxel visibility and shadow
contours directly from the modulo-24 clock phase interference on the observer cross.
"""

import os
import sys
import subprocess
import numpy as np

class PTRCGameWorld:
    """ Defines the physical game space: walls, pyramid, and flowing fluid. """
    def __init__(self, size=64):
        self.size = size
        # 3D spatial grid: 0 = vacuum, 1 = pipe wall, 2 = pyramid, 3 = active fluid
        self.voxels = np.zeros((size, size, size), dtype=np.uint8)
        self._build_world_geometry()

    def _build_world_geometry(self):
        mid = self.size // 2
        
        # 1. HORIZONTAL FLUID CHANNEL: Tightened boundary walls for solid visibility
        for x in range(self.size):
            for y in range(self.size):
                for z in range(self.size):
                    dx = x - mid
                    dy = y - mid
                    r_squared = dx*dx + dy*dy
                    if r_squared >= 81 and r_squared <= 121:
                        self.voxels[x, y, z] = 1

        # 2. SECTORED GOLD PYRAMID: Enhanced monolith volume in the absolute core
        for h in range(16):
            span = 14 - h
            for dx in range(-span, span + 1):
                for dy in range(-span, span + 1):
                    px = mid + dx
                    py = mid + dy
                    pz = mid - 8 + h
                    if 0 <= px < self.size and 0 <= py < self.size and 0 <= pz < self.size:
                        if self.voxels[px, py, pz] == 0:
                            self.voxels[px, py, pz] = 2

    def update_fluid_flow(self, frame_idx):
        """ Clocks the fluid resonance with orthogonal velocity vector translation. """
        mid = self.size // 2
        
        # Fluid injection along the visible Z-axis pipeline entrance
        takt_step = frame_idx % 4
        if takt_step == 0:
            self.voxels[mid-2:mid+3, mid-2:mid+3, 2:6] = 3

        # Volumetric vector shift pushing the cyan fluid forward and around the gold core
        for z in range(self.size - 1, -1, -1):
            for y in range(self.size):
                for x in range(self.size):
                    if self.voxels[x, y, z] == 3:
                        if z + 1 < self.size:
                            next_voxel = self.voxels[x, y, z + 1]
                            if next_voxel == 0:
                                self.voxels[x, y, z + 1] = 3
                                self.voxels[x, y, z] = 0
                            elif next_voxel in (1, 2):  # Collision deflection
                                deflected = False
                                for dx in (-1, 1):
                                    for dy in (-1, 1):
                                        if 0 <= x+dx < self.size and 0 <= y+dy < self.size:
                                            if self.voxels[x+dx, y+dy, z] == 0:
                                                self.voxels[x+dx, y+dy, z] = 3
                                                self.voxels[x, y, z] = 0
                                                deflected = True
                                                break
                                    if deflected: break
                                if not deflected:
                                    self.voxels[x, y, z] = 0

class PTRCTrueCrystalSieve:
    def __init__(self, v_scale=9):
        self.v_scale = int(v_scale)
        self.crystal_axes = self._build_144_facet_vectors()

    def _build_144_facet_vectors(self):
        flat_nodes = [
            2, 1, 0,  2, -1, 0, -2,  1, 0, -2, -1, 0,
            0, 2, 1,  0,  2, -1,  0, -2, 1,  0, -2, -1,
            1, 0, 2, -1,  0,  2,  1,  0, -2, -1,  0, -2
        ]
        base_tensors = np.array(flat_nodes, dtype=np.int32).reshape(12, 3)
        axes = []
        for node in base_tensors:
            for factor in range(1, 13):
                axes.append(node * factor)
        return np.array(axes, dtype=np.int32)

class PTRCGameViewport:
    def __init__(self, width=1920, height=1080):
        self.width = width
        self.height = height
        self.SCALE = 1000

    def render_to_screen(self, world_instance, frame_idx):
        """ Projects the 3D space utilizing crossed mirror phase interference for shadows. """
        frame_buffer = np.zeros((self.height, self.width, 3), dtype=np.uint8)
        z_buffer = np.full((self.height, self.width), fill_value=999999, dtype=np.int32)
        
        cx, cy = self.width // 2, self.height // 2
        w_size = world_instance.size
        w_mid = w_size // 2

        # Controlled camera sweep matching the 144-facet vector routing
        angle_takt = int((frame_idx % 24) - 12)

        for x in range(w_size):
            for y in range(w_size):
                for z in range(w_size):
                    voxel_type = world_instance.voxels[x, y, z]
                    if voxel_type == 0:
                        continue

                    # 1:1 INT MAP WITH HIGHER DENSITY OVERLAP
                    rx = (x - w_mid) * 24
                    ry = (y - w_mid) * 24
                    rz = (z - w_mid) * 24

                    # VOLUMETRIC VIEWPORT PROJECTION MATRIX
                    rotated_x = rx + (rz * angle_takt) // 24
                    rotated_y = ry + (rz * angle_takt) // 24
                    rotated_z = rz + (rx * angle_takt) // 24 + 180

                    if rotated_z <= 10:
                        continue

                    # Lossless perspective transformation via 1414 invariant
                    out_x = (((rotated_x * 45 * self.SCALE) // ((rotated_z * self.SCALE) // 1414)) + cx) % self.width
                    out_y = (((rotated_y * 45 * self.SCALE) // ((rotated_z * self.SCALE) // 1414)) + cy) % self.height

                    if rotated_z < z_buffer[out_y, out_x]:
                        z_buffer[out_y, out_x] = rotated_z

                        if voxel_type == 1:
                            r, g, b = 150, 155, 170  # Steel Gray (Boundary)
                        elif voxel_type == 2:
                            r, g, b = 255, 200, 0    # Massive Gold (Monolith Pyramid)
                        else:
                            r, g, b = 0, 255, 240    # High-emission Cyan (Fluid)

                        # THE ERLEUCHTUNG: Mirror-Cross shadow modulation via Modulo-24 clock phase
                        # Visibility and contour brightness are calculated strictly from the time sieve!
                        mirror_resonance = (x * y + frame_idx) % 24
                        mirror_shading = 255 - (mirror_resonance * 8) # Pure rest-class subtraction

                        # DENSE SOLID FILL: Fully interlocking facet matrix to close the monitor gaps
                        crystal_structure = [
                            (0, 0), (0, -1), (0, 1), (-1, 0), (1, 0),
                            (0, -2), (0, 2), (-2, 0), (2, 0),
                            (-1, -1), (1, -1), (-1, 1), (1, 1),
                            (-2, -1), (2, -1), (-2, 1), (2, 1),
                            (-1, -2), (1, -2), (-1, 2), (1, 2),
                            (-2, -2), (2, -2), (-2, 2), (2, 2)
                        ]
                        
                        for ox, oy in crystal_structure:
                            px = (out_x + ox) % self.width
                            py = (out_y + oy) % self.height
                            
                            if ox == 0 and oy == 0:
                                # Core crystal node shaded strictly by crossed mirror intercept
                                frame_buffer[py, px] = [
                                    (r * mirror_shading) // 255,
                                    (g * mirror_shading) // 255,
                                    (b * mirror_shading) // 255
                                ]
                            else:
                                # Secondary facets shaded harmonically under the mirror rhythm
                                frame_buffer[py, px] = [
                                    (r * mirror_shading) // 380,
                                    (g * mirror_shading) // 380,
                                    (b * mirror_shading) // 380
                                ]

        return frame_buffer

if __name__ == "__main__":
    print("[pTRC-VIEWPORT] Switching graphics pipeline to 2D monitor projection (Demo Mode)...")
    
    base_dir = os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))
    cache_dir = os.path.join(base_dir, "Stabilized_Cache")
    os.makedirs(cache_dir, exist_ok=True)

    android_download_dir = "/storage/emulated/0/Download"
    video_output_dir = android_download_dir if os.path.exists(android_download_dir) else base_dir
    os.makedirs(video_output_dir, exist_ok=True)

    total_frames = 144
    world = PTRCGameWorld(size=64)
    viewport = PTRCGameViewport(width=1920, height=1080)

    for frame in range(total_frames):
        world.update_fluid_flow(frame)
        frame_matrix = viewport.render_to_screen(world, frame)
        
        filename = os.path.join(cache_dir, f"game_frame_{frame:04d}.ppm")
        header = f"P6\n{viewport.width} {viewport.height}\n255\n".encode('ascii')
        with open(filename, 'wb') as f:
            f.write(header)
            f.write(frame_matrix.tobytes())

        if frame % 24 == 0 or frame == total_frames - 1:
            print(f" + Viewport frame {frame:03d}/{total_frames} z-buffered and rasterized.")

    input_pattern = os.path.join(cache_dir, "game_frame_%04d.ppm")
    video_target_path = os.path.join(video_output_dir, "ptrc_game_simulation_v001.mp4")

    version = 1
    while os.path.exists(video_target_path):
        video_target_path = os.path.join(video_output_dir, f"ptrc_game_simulation_v{version:03d}.mp4")
        version += 1

    print("\n -> Compiling plastic 2D monitor stream via FFmpeg...")
    ffmpeg_cmd = [
        "ffmpeg", "-y", "-framerate", "24", "-i", input_pattern,
        "-c:v", "libx264", "-crf", "0", "-preset", "slow", "-pix_fmt", "yuv420p", video_target_path
    ]

    try:
        subprocess.run(ffmpeg_cmd, capture_output=True, check=True)
        print(f"\n[ SYSTEM CHECK OK ] The unified game video has been successfully secured!")
        print(f" -> File: {video_target_path}")

        for frame in range(total_frames):
            ppm_file = os.path.join(cache_dir, f"game_frame_{frame:04d}.ppm")
            if os.path.exists(ppm_file): os.remove(ppm_file)
        os.rmdir(cache_dir)
        print(" + Viewport cache atomically cleared. Invariance fully preserved.")
        sys.exit(0)

    except Exception as e:
        print(f"\n[ WARNING ] FFmpeg video compilation failed: {e}")
        sys.exit(1)

# -*- coding: utf-8 -*-
"""
pTRC Framework - Static Crystal Geometry Sieve
Operational Domain: Pure Discrete Memory Chains (\mathbb{Z}^3) for zero-space formclosure.
"""

class PTRCGeometrySieve:
    """
    Berechnet die statischen Raumpixel-Zentren für das 13-Sphären-Gitter
    und die raumzentrierte Pyramide im unmanifestierten Ganzzahlfeld.
    """
    def __init__(self, cx, cy, cz, v_scale=14):
        self.cx = int(cx)
        self.cy = int(cy)
        self.cz = int(cz)
        self.v_scale = int(v_scale)

    def generate_monolith_nodes(self, frame_idx):
        """Gibt ortsfeste, ruhig im Raum stehende Kristallstrukturen zurück."""
        kristall_zentren = []
        
        # Frames 0 bis 71: Die vollkommen ruhige Wirminghaus-Kugel-Ur-Zelle
        if frame_idx < 72:
            fcc_sphere = [
                (0, 0, 0),
                (2, 2, 0), (-2, 2, 0), (2, -2, 0), (-2, -2, 0),
                (2, 0, 2), (2, 0, -2), (-2, 0, 2), (-2, 0, -2),
                (0, 2, 2), (0, 2, -2), (0, -2, 2), (0, -2, -2)
            ]
            for k_x, k_y, k_z in fcc_sphere:
                kx = self.cx + (k_x * self.v_scale)
                ky = self.cy + (k_y * self.v_scale)
                kz = self.cz + (k_z * self.v_scale)
                kristall_zentren.append((kx, ky, kz))
                
        # Frames 72 bis 143: Die unbewegliche, massive Pyramide aus Raumkristallen
        else:
            pyramid_nodes = [
                (-2, 2, -2), (2, 2, -2), (2, -2, -2), (-2, -2, -2), # Fundament
                (-1, 1, 0), (1, 1, 0), (1, -1, 0), (-1, -1, 0),     # Mittelstufe
                (0, 0, 2)                                           # Kristallspitze
            ]
            for k_x, k_y, k_z in pyramid_nodes:
                kx = self.cx + (k_x * self.v_scale)
                ky = self.cy + (k_y * self.v_scale) + 12            # Zentrierungskompensation
                kz = self.cz + (k_z * self.v_scale)
                kristall_zentren.append((kx, ky, kz))
                
        return kristall_zentren
